+/-- A five-cycle is a cycle: `IsFiveCycle σ → σ.IsCycle` (`Equiv.Perm.card_cycleType_eq_one`).
+Glue for C10. -/
+private theorem IsFiveCycle.isCycle {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : IsFiveCycle σ) : σ.IsCycle := by
+  rw [isFiveCycle_iff] at h
+  rw [← Perm.card_cycleType_eq_one, h, Multiset.card_singleton]

+/-- A five-cycle has order five: `IsFiveCycle σ → orderOf σ = 5` (`Equiv.Perm.lcm_cycleType`).
+Glue for C10. -/
+private theorem IsFiveCycle.orderOf_eq {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : IsFiveCycle σ) :
+    orderOf σ = 5 := by
+  rw [isFiveCycle_iff] at h
+  rw [← Perm.lcm_cycleType, h, Multiset.lcm_singleton, normalize_eq]

+/-- A five-cycle on `Fin 5` moves every point: `IsFiveCycle σ → σ i ≠ i` (its support has
+`5 = Fintype.card (Fin 5)` elements, `Equiv.Perm.sum_cycleType`).  Glue for C10. -/
+private theorem IsFiveCycle.apply_ne {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : IsFiveCycle σ) (i : Fin 5) :
+    σ i ≠ i := by
+  rw [isFiveCycle_iff] at h
+  have hs : σ.support = Finset.univ := by
+    apply Finset.eq_univ_of_card
+    rw [← Perm.sum_cycleType, h, Multiset.sum_singleton, Fintype.card_fin]
+  rw [← Perm.mem_support, hs]
+  exact Finset.mem_univ i
+
+/-- On the orbit of `0` under a five-cycle, powers agree exactly modulo `5`:
+`IsFiveCycle σ → ((σ ^ a) 0 = (σ ^ b) 0 ↔ a ≡ b [MOD 5])` (`Equiv.Perm.IsCycle.pow_eq_pow_iff`,
+`pow_eq_pow_iff_modEq`).  Glue for C10 [Bar89, Lemma 1 proof, p003-b003]. -/
+private theorem IsFiveCycle.pow_apply_eq_iff {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : IsFiveCycle σ) {a b : ℕ} :
+    (σ ^ a) 0 = (σ ^ b) 0 ↔ a ≡ b [MOD 5] := by
+  have h5 := h.orderOf_eq
+  constructor
+  · intro hab
+    have hmod := pow_eq_pow_iff_modEq.1 (h.isCycle.pow_eq_pow_iff.2 ⟨0, h.apply_ne 0, hab⟩)
+    rwa [h5] at hmod
+  · intro hab
+    rw [← h5] at hab
+    rw [pow_eq_pow_iff_modEq.2 hab]
+
+/-- For a five-cycle `σ`, `conjugatorIndex σ i` is an exponent reaching `i` from `0`:
+`IsFiveCycle σ → (σ ^ conjugatorIndex σ i) 0 = i`.  Some `k < orderOf σ = 5` reaches `i`
+(`Equiv.Perm.SameCycle.exists_pow_eq'`), so `List.find?` succeeds on `List.range 5`.  Glue for
+C10 [Bar89, Lemma 1 proof, p003-b003]. -/
+private theorem pow_conjugatorIndex {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : IsFiveCycle σ) (i : Fin 5) :
+    (σ ^ conjugatorIndex σ i) 0 = i := by
+  obtain ⟨k, hk, hki⟩ := (h.isCycle.sameCycle (h.apply_ne 0) (h.apply_ne i)).exists_pow_eq'
+  rw [h.orderOf_eq] at hk
+  rw [conjugatorIndex_eq]
+  cases hf : (List.range 5).find? (fun k => (σ ^ k) 0 = i) with
+  | none =>
+    rw [List.find?_eq_none] at hf
+    exact absurd (decide_eq_true hki) (hf k (List.mem_range.2 hk))
+  | some m =>
+    rw [Option.getD_some]
+    have hm := List.find?_some (p := fun k => decide ((σ ^ k) 0 = i)) hf
+    exact of_decide_eq_true hm

+/-- A one-instruction program `⟨[⟨i, σ₀, σ₁⟩]⟩` yields `σ₁` on `x i = 1` and `σ₀` on `x i = 0`:
+`(⟨[Instr.ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁]⟩ : BranchingProgram 5 n).yield x = if x i then ⇑σ₁ else ⇑σ₀`.  Glue for
+the leaves of C13 (`yield_eq`, `Instr.eval_eq`, the `Instr.ofPerm` field readers). -/
+private theorem yield_ofPerm (i : Fin n) (σ₀ σ₁ : Perm (Fin 5)) (x : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (⟨[Instr.ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁]⟩ : BranchingProgram 5 n).yield x = if x i then ⇑σ₁ else ⇑σ₀ := by
+  simp only [yield_eq, List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_nil, Function.comp_id]
+  rw [Instr.eval_eq, Instr.ofPerm_var, funext (Instr.ofPerm_map0 i σ₀ σ₁),
+    funext (Instr.ofPerm_map1 i σ₀ σ₁)]

+/-- A one-instruction program has length one:
+`(⟨[Instr.ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁]⟩ : BranchingProgram 5 n).length = 1`.  Glue for C14. -/
+private theorem length_ofPerm (i : Fin n) (σ₀ σ₁ : Perm (Fin 5)) :
+    (⟨[Instr.ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁]⟩ : BranchingProgram 5 n).length = 1 := by
+  simp only [length_eq, List.length_singleton]

+/-- A one-instruction program built from permutations is a permutation program:
+`(⟨[Instr.ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁]⟩ : BranchingProgram 5 n).IsPermutation` (`Instr.isPermutation_ofPerm`).
+Glue for C15. -/
+private theorem isPermutation_ofPerm (i : Fin n) (σ₀ σ₁ : Perm (Fin 5)) :
+    (⟨[Instr.ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁]⟩ : BranchingProgram 5 n).IsPermutation := by
+  rw [isPermutation_iff]
+  intro ι hι
+  simp only [List.mem_singleton] at hι
+  subst hι
+  exact (Instr.isPermutation_iff _).1 (Instr.isPermutation_ofPerm i σ₀ σ₁)

+/-- A five-cycle recognizer's yield as a permutation: if `B` five-cycle recognizes `A` with output
+`σ`, then `B.yield x = ⇑(if A x then σ else 1)`.  Glue for the `∧` step of C13. -/
+private theorem FiveCycleRecognizes.yield_eq_ite {B : BranchingProgram 5 n}
+    {A : (Fin n → Bool) → Prop} {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : FiveCycleRecognizes B A σ)
+    (x : Fin n → Bool) [Decidable (A x)] : B.yield x = ⇑(if A x then σ else 1) := by
+  rw [fiveCycleRecognizes_iff] at h
+  by_cases hA : A x
+  · rw [if_pos hA, (h.2 x).1 hA]
+  · rw [if_neg hA, (h.2 x).2 hA, Perm.coe_one]
+
+/-- Five-cycle recognition only depends on the recognized set: if `B` five-cycle recognizes `A`
+with output `σ` and `A x ↔ A' x` for all `x`, then it recognizes `A'`.  Glue for C13. -/
+private theorem FiveCycleRecognizes.of_iff {B : BranchingProgram 5 n}
+    {A A' : (Fin n → Bool) → Prop} {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (h : FiveCycleRecognizes B A σ)
+    (hA : ∀ x, A x ↔ A' x) : FiveCycleRecognizes B A' σ := by
+  have hAA : A = A' := funext fun x => propext (hA x)
+  subst hAA
+  exact h
+
+/-- **The `∧` step** of [Bar89, Thm 1 proof, p004-b002]: if `P₁ ρ` and `P₂ ρ` five-cycle recognize
+`A₁` and `A₂` with output `ρ` for every five-cycle `ρ`, then `commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ`
+recognizes `A₁ ∩ A₂` with output `σ`.  On `A₁ ∩ A₂` the yield is `τ₂⁻¹ τ₁⁻¹ τ₂ τ₁ =
+θ (sigma2⁻¹ sigma1⁻¹ sigma2 sigma1) θ⁻¹ = σ` (`isFiveCycle_commutator`, `conjugator_conj`); off it
+two of the four blocks are the identity and the other two cancel.  A named step of C13's
+sketch.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Write `θ` for the conjugator and `τᵢ = θ * sigmaᵢ * θ⁻¹`, five-cycles by
+`Equiv.Perm.cycleType_conj`, as are their inverses.  By `yield_append` the yield is the product of
+the four blocks' yields, and each block's yield is `τ` or `1` according to its recognized set.
+Split on `A₁ x` and `A₂ x`: if both hold the product `τ₂⁻¹ τ₁⁻¹ τ₂ τ₁` equals
+`θ (sigma2⁻¹ sigma1⁻¹ sigma2 sigma1) θ⁻¹`, which is `σ` by `conjugator_conj` (group identities);
+otherwise the surviving blocks are `τ⁻¹` and `τ` for the same `τ`, or none, and cancel. -/
+private theorem commutatorProgram_recognizes {P₁ P₂ : Perm (Fin 5) → BranchingProgram 5 n}
+    {A₁ A₂ : (Fin n → Bool) → Prop}
+    (h₁ : ∀ ρ, IsFiveCycle ρ → FiveCycleRecognizes (P₁ ρ) A₁ ρ)
+    (h₂ : ∀ ρ, IsFiveCycle ρ → FiveCycleRecognizes (P₂ ρ) A₂ ρ)
+    {σ : Perm (Fin 5)} (hσ : IsFiveCycle σ) :
+    FiveCycleRecognizes (commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ) (fun x => A₁ x ∧ A₂ x) σ := by
+  classical
+  set θ := conjugator (sigma2⁻¹ * sigma1⁻¹ * sigma2 * sigma1) σ with hθ
+  have hc₁ : IsFiveCycle (θ * sigma1 * θ⁻¹) := isFiveCycle_conj isFiveCycle_sigma1 θ
+  have hc₂ : IsFiveCycle (θ * sigma2 * θ⁻¹) := isFiveCycle_conj isFiveCycle_sigma2 θ
+  have hθσ : θ * (sigma2⁻¹ * sigma1⁻¹ * sigma2 * sigma1) * θ⁻¹ = σ :=
+    conjugator_conj isFiveCycle_commutator hσ
+  -- the yield, as a product of the four blocks' permutations
+  have key : ∀ x, (commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ).yield x = ⇑(if A₁ x ∧ A₂ x then σ else 1) := by
+    intro x
+    rw [commutatorProgram_eq, ← hθ, yield_append, yield_append, yield_append,
+      (h₁ _ hc₁).yield_eq_ite x, (h₂ _ hc₂).yield_eq_ite x,
+      (h₁ _ (isFiveCycle_inv hc₁)).yield_eq_ite x, (h₂ _ (isFiveCycle_inv hc₂)).yield_eq_ite x,
+      ← Perm.coe_mul, ← Perm.coe_mul, ← Perm.coe_mul]
+    congr 1
+    -- off `A₁ ∩ A₂` the blocks cancel; on it the product is the conjugated commutator
+    by_cases ha₁ : A₁ x <;> by_cases ha₂ : A₂ x <;>
+      simp only [ha₁, ha₂, and_self, and_true, and_false, ↓reduceIte,
+        one_mul, mul_one, inv_mul_cancel]
+    rw [← hθσ]
+    group
+  rw [fiveCycleRecognizes_iff]
+  refine ⟨hσ, fun x => ⟨fun hA => ?_, fun hA => ?_⟩⟩
+  · rw [key, if_pos hA]
+  · rw [key, if_neg hA, Perm.coe_one]
+
+/-- A commutator program is nonempty when its first block is: `(∀ ρ, 0 < (P₁ ρ).length) →
+0 < (commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ).length` (`commutatorProgram_eq`, `length_append`).  Glue for
+C13 and C14. -/
+private theorem commutatorProgram_length_pos {P₁ P₂ : Perm (Fin 5) → BranchingProgram 5 n}
+    (h₁ : ∀ ρ, 0 < (P₁ ρ).length) (σ : Perm (Fin 5)) :
+    0 < (commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ).length := by
+  set θ := conjugator (sigma2⁻¹ * sigma1⁻¹ * sigma2 * sigma1) σ with hθ
+  rw [commutatorProgram_eq, ← hθ, length_append, length_append, length_append]
+  have := h₁ (θ * sigma1 * θ⁻¹)
+  omega
+
+/-- A commutator program of blocks of length at most `K` has length at most `4 · K`:
+`(∀ ρ, (P₁ ρ).length ≤ K) → (∀ ρ, (P₂ ρ).length ≤ K) → (commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ).length ≤ 4 * K`
+(`commutatorProgram_eq`, `length_append`) [Bar89, Thm 1 proof, p004-b002].  Glue for C14. -/
+private theorem length_commutatorProgram_le {P₁ P₂ : Perm (Fin 5) → BranchingProgram 5 n}
+    {K : ℕ} (h₁ : ∀ ρ, (P₁ ρ).length ≤ K) (h₂ : ∀ ρ, (P₂ ρ).length ≤ K) (σ : Perm (Fin 5)) :
+    (commutatorProgram P₁ P₂ σ).length ≤ 4 * K := by
+  set θ := conjugator (sigma2⁻¹ * sigma1⁻¹ * sigma2 * sigma1) σ with hθ
+  rw [commutatorProgram_eq, ← hθ, length_append, length_append, length_append]
+  have a := h₁ (θ * sigma1 * θ⁻¹)
+  have b := h₂ (θ * sigma2 * θ⁻¹)
+  have c := h₁ (θ * sigma1 * θ⁻¹)⁻¹
+  have d := h₂ (θ * sigma2 * θ⁻¹)⁻¹
+  omega
+
+/-- Every Barrington program is nonempty: `0 < (barrington c σ).length`.  The proof of C14's
+`barrington_length_pos`, placed before C13, which needs it for Lemma 2 in the `∨` case.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induction on `c` (`TreeCircuit.ind`) generalizing `σ`, by the readers of
+`barrington`: literals and constants have one instruction; a fan-in-1 node is its child's
+program; the `∧` and `∨` programs contain the first child's program as a block
+(`commutatorProgram_length_pos`; `length_complementLast` for the `∨` case's complements). -/
+private theorem barrington_length_pos_aux [NeZero n] (c : TreeCircuit n) (σ : Perm (Fin 5)) :
+    0 < (barrington c σ).length := by
+  refine TreeCircuit.ind (motive := fun c => ∀ σ, 0 < (barrington c σ).length) ?_ ?_ c σ
+  · rintro ⟨i, s⟩ σ
+    cases s
+    · rw [barrington_lit_false, length_ofPerm]
+      exact Nat.one_pos
+    · rw [barrington_lit_true, length_ofPerm]
+      exact Nat.one_pos
+  · intro b cs ih σ
+    rcases cs with _ | ⟨c₁, _ | ⟨c₂, cs⟩⟩
+    · cases b
+      · rw [barrington_node_false_nil, length_ofPerm]
+        exact Nat.one_pos
+      · rw [barrington_node_true_nil, length_ofPerm]
+        exact Nat.one_pos
+    · rw [barrington_node_singleton]
+      exact ih c₁ (List.mem_singleton.2 rfl) σ
+    · have h₁ := ih c₁ (List.mem_cons.2 (Or.inl rfl))
+      cases b
+      · rw [barrington_node_false_cons_cons, length_complementLast]
+        exact commutatorProgram_length_pos
+          (P₁ := fun ρ => (barrington c₁ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹)
+          (fun ρ => by simp only [length_complementLast]; exact h₁ ρ⁻¹) σ⁻¹
+      · rw [barrington_node_true_cons_cons]
+        exact commutatorProgram_length_pos (P₁ := fun ρ => barrington c₁ ρ) h₁ σ

+          intro c hc
+          exact absurd hc List.not_mem_nil
+        rw [barrington_node_true_nil, yield_ofPerm, ite_self]
+        exact ⟨fun _ => rfl, fun h => absurd ht h⟩
+    · -- fan-in 1: the child's program
+      have hm : c₁ ∈ [c₁] := List.mem_singleton.2 rfl
+      rw [barrington_node_singleton]
+      refine (ih c₁ hm (hch c₁ hm) σ hσ).of_iff fun x => ?_
+      cases b
+      · rw [TreeCircuit.eval_node_false_iff]
+        exact ⟨fun h => ⟨c₁, hm, h⟩, fun ⟨c, hc, h⟩ => List.mem_singleton.1 hc ▸ h⟩
+      · rw [TreeCircuit.eval_node_true_iff]
+        exact ⟨fun h c hc => List.mem_singleton.1 hc ▸ h, fun h => h c₁ hm⟩
+    · -- fan-in 2
+      have hm₁ : c₁ ∈ [c₁, c₂] := List.mem_cons.2 (Or.inl rfl)
+      have hm₂ : c₂ ∈ [c₁, c₂] := List.mem_cons.2 (Or.inr (List.mem_singleton.2 rfl))
+      have hmem : ∀ c, c ∈ [c₁, c₂] → c = c₁ ∨ c = c₂ := fun c hc => by
+        rcases List.mem_cons.1 hc with h | h
+        · exact Or.inl h
+        · exact Or.inr (List.mem_singleton.1 h)
+      have ih₁ : ∀ ρ, IsFiveCycle ρ →
+          FiveCycleRecognizes (barrington c₁ ρ) (fun x => c₁.eval x = true) ρ :=
+        fun ρ hρ => ih c₁ hm₁ (hch c₁ hm₁) ρ hρ
+      have ih₂ : ∀ ρ, IsFiveCycle ρ →
+          FiveCycleRecognizes (barrington c₂ ρ) (fun x => c₂.eval x = true) ρ :=
+        fun ρ hρ => ih c₂ hm₂ (hch c₂ hm₂) ρ hρ
+      cases b
+      · -- `∨`: De Morgan through Lemma 2 (C12)
+        have hn₁ : ∀ ρ, IsFiveCycle ρ →
+            FiveCycleRecognizes ((barrington c₁ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹)
+              (fun x => ¬ c₁.eval x = true) ρ := by
+          intro ρ hρ
+          have h := (ih₁ ρ⁻¹ (isFiveCycle_inv hρ)).complementLast
+            (barrington_length_pos_aux c₁ ρ⁻¹)
+          rwa [inv_inv] at h
+        have hn₂ : ∀ ρ, IsFiveCycle ρ →
+            FiveCycleRecognizes ((barrington c₂ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹)
+              (fun x => ¬ c₂.eval x = true) ρ := by
+          intro ρ hρ
+          have h := (ih₂ ρ⁻¹ (isFiveCycle_inv hρ)).complementLast
+            (barrington_length_pos_aux c₂ ρ⁻¹)
+          rwa [inv_inv] at h
+        have hand := commutatorProgram_recognizes
+          (P₁ := fun ρ => (barrington c₁ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹)
+          (P₂ := fun ρ => (barrington c₂ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹) hn₁ hn₂ (isFiveCycle_inv hσ)
+        have hpos := commutatorProgram_length_pos
+          (P₁ := fun ρ => (barrington c₁ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹)
+          (P₂ := fun ρ => (barrington c₂ ρ⁻¹).complementLast ρ⁻¹)
+          (fun ρ => by
+            simp only [length_complementLast]
+            exact barrington_length_pos_aux c₁ ρ⁻¹) σ⁻¹
+        have hor := hand.complementLast hpos
+        rw [inv_inv] at hor
+        rw [barrington_node_false_cons_cons]
+        refine hor.of_iff fun x => ?_
+        dsimp only
+        rw [TreeCircuit.eval_node_false_iff]
+        constructor
+        · intro h
+          by_contra h'
+          exact h ⟨fun h₁ => h' ⟨c₁, hm₁, h₁⟩, fun h₂ => h' ⟨c₂, hm₂, h₂⟩⟩
+        · rintro ⟨c, hc, hce⟩ ⟨h₁, h₂⟩
+          rcases hmem c hc with rfl | rfl
+          · exact h₁ hce
+          · exact h₂ hce
+      · -- `∧`: the commutator step
+        rw [barrington_node_true_cons_cons]
+        refine (commutatorProgram_recognizes (P₁ := fun ρ => barrington c₁ ρ)
+          (P₂ := fun ρ => barrington c₂ ρ) ih₁ ih₂ hσ).of_iff fun x => ?_
+        dsimp only
+        rw [TreeCircuit.eval_node_true_iff]
+        constructor
+        · rintro ⟨h₁, h₂⟩ c hc
+          rcases hmem c hc with rfl | rfl
+          · exact h₁
+          · exact h₂
+        · intro h
+          exact ⟨h c₁ hm₁, h c₂ hm₂⟩
+    · -- fan-in above 2 contradicts `hc`
+      simp only [List.length_cons] at hlen
+      omega

 /-- **C18.** One composition circuit composes: if the wires `F` represent `f x` and the wires `G`

 /-- **C18.** A composition circuit adds `⌈log₂ w⌉ + 2` levels: if all wires of `F` and `G` have

+`List.length_finRange`), so the depth is `≤ (d + 1) + ⌈log₂ w⌉ + 1`. -/

     (hF : ∀ i j, (F i j).depth ≤ d) (hG : ∀ i j, (G i j).depth ≤ d) (i k : Fin w) :

+    have h4 := hG j k

+/-- A composition circuit of fan-in-2 wires has fan-in 2: if `∀ i j, (F i j).maxFanin ≤ 2` and
+`∀ i j, (G i j).maxFanin ≤ 2`, then `(wireComp F G i k).maxFanin ≤ 2`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem maxFanin_wireComp_le {F G : Fin w → Fin w → TreeCircuit n}
+    (hF : ∀ i j, (F i j).maxFanin ≤ 2) (hG : ∀ i j, (G i j).maxFanin ≤ 2) (i k : Fin w) :
+    (wireComp F G i k).maxFanin ≤ 2 := by

+  simp only [← TreeCircuit.and2_eq]
+  refine (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_balanced_le _ _).trans

+  obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+  have h2 := TreeCircuit.maxFanin_and2_le (F i j) (G j k)
+  have h3 := hF i j
+  have h4 := hG j k
+  omega

+/-- The composition tree of an instruction list `l` computes the yield of the program `⟨l⟩`:
+`(compTree l i j).eval x = decide ((⟨l⟩ : BranchingProgram w n).yield x i = j)`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Well-founded recursion on `l.length`, following `compTree`.  Empty list:
+`compTree_nil`, `const_eq`/`const_eval` and `yield_nil`.  One instruction: `compTree_singleton`,
+`Instr.wire_eval`, and `yield_eq` with `List.foldl` on `[ι]`.  Otherwise (Step 1) `⟨l⟩` is
+`⟨l.take m⟩ ++ ⟨l.drop m⟩` (`append_instrs`, `List.take_append_drop`), so (Step 2)
+`compTree_cons_cons`, `yield_append` and `wireComp_eval` with the recursive calls close it. -/
+private theorem compTree_eval_list :
+    ∀ (l : List (Instr w n)) (x : Fin n → Bool) (i j : Fin w),
+      (compTree l i j).eval x = decide ((⟨l⟩ : BranchingProgram w n).yield x i = j)
+  | [], x, i, j => by
+    rw [compTree_nil, yield_nil, id_eq]
+    by_cases h : i = j
+    · rw [if_pos h, ← TreeCircuit.const_eq, TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+      exact (decide_eq_true h).symm
+    · rw [if_neg h, ← TreeCircuit.const_eq, TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+      exact (decide_eq_false h).symm
+  | [ι], x, i, j => by
+    have hy : (⟨[ι]⟩ : BranchingProgram w n).yield x = ι.eval x := by
+      simp only [yield_eq, List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_nil, Function.comp_id]
+    rw [compTree_singleton, Instr.wire_eval, hy]
+  | ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l, x, i, j => by
+    -- Step 1: the program is the concatenation of its two halves.
+    have hsplit : ∀ (m : ℕ) (l' : List (Instr w n)),
+        (⟨l'⟩ : BranchingProgram w n) = ⟨l'.take m⟩ ++ ⟨l'.drop m⟩ := by
+      intro m l'
+      have h : (⟨l'.take m⟩ ++ ⟨l'.drop m⟩ : BranchingProgram w n).instrs = l' := by
+        rw [append_instrs]
+        exact List.take_append_drop m l'
+      exact (congrArg BranchingProgram.mk h).symm
+    -- Step 2: compose the two halves.
+    rw [compTree_cons_cons, hsplit ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length / 2) (ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l), yield_append]
+    exact wireComp_eval
+      (fun x i j => compTree_eval_list ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).take ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length / 2)) x i j)
+      (fun x i j => compTree_eval_list ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).drop ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length / 2)) x i j)
+      i j x
+termination_by l => l.length
+decreasing_by
+  all_goals simp only [List.length_take, List.length_drop, List.length_cons]
+  all_goals omega
 
 /-- **C19.** The composition tree computes the yield: `(compTree B.instrs i j).eval x =
 decide (B.yield x i = j)` [Bar89, Thm 2 proof, p004-b003].
 
-**Proof sketch.** Strong induction on the length of an instruction list `l`, for the yield of
-`⟨l⟩`: the empty list by `compTree_nil` and `yield_nil`; one instruction by `compTree_singleton`,
+**Proof sketch.** The helper `compTree_eval_list`, for an arbitrary instruction list `l` and the
+yield of `⟨l⟩`, by well-founded recursion on the length of `l` (as `compTree` itself): the empty
+list by `compTree_nil`, `const_eval` and `yield_nil`; one instruction by `compTree_singleton`,
 `Instr.wire_eval` and `yield_eq`; otherwise `compTree_cons_cons`, `wireComp_eval` with the
-induction hypothesis for both halves, and `yield_append` on `l = l.take m ++ l.drop m`
-(`List.take_append_drop`, `append_instrs`). -/
+recursive calls for both halves, and `yield_append` on `⟨l⟩ = ⟨l.take m⟩ ++ ⟨l.drop m⟩`
+(`List.take_append_drop`, `append_instrs`); then `l = B.instrs`. -/
 theorem compTree_eval (B : BranchingProgram w n) (i j : Fin w) (x : Fin n → Bool) :
     (compTree B.instrs i j).eval x = decide (B.yield x i = j) := by
-  sorry
+  exact compTree_eval_list B.instrs x i j
+
+/-- The composition tree of an instruction list `l` has depth logarithmic in `|l|`:
+`(compTree l i j).depth ≤ (Nat.clog 2 w + 2) * Nat.clog 2 l.length + 1`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Well-founded recursion on `l.length`, following `compTree`.  The leaves have
+depth `≤ 1` (`depth_const`, `Instr.depth_wire_le`).  For `L = |l| ≥ 2`: (Step 1)
+`⌈log₂ L⌉ = ⌈log₂ ⌈L/2⌉⌉ + 1` (`Nat.clog_of_two_le`); (Step 2) both halves have length
+`≤ ⌈L/2⌉` (`List.length_take`, `List.length_drop`), so by the recursive calls and
+`Nat.clog_mono_right` their wires have depth `≤ (⌈log₂ w⌉ + 2)·⌈log₂ ⌈L/2⌉⌉ + 1`; (Step 3)
+`depth_wireComp_le` adds `⌈log₂ w⌉ + 2`. -/
+private theorem depth_compTree_list_le :
+    ∀ (l : List (Instr w n)) (i j : Fin w),
+      (compTree l i j).depth ≤ (Nat.clog 2 w + 2) * Nat.clog 2 l.length + 1
+  | [], i, j => by
+    rw [compTree_nil]
+    split_ifs <;>
+      (rw [← TreeCircuit.const_eq, TreeCircuit.depth_const]; exact Nat.le_add_left 1 _)
+  | [ι], i, j => by
+    rw [compTree_singleton]
+    exact (Instr.depth_wire_le ι i j).trans (Nat.le_add_left 1 _)
+  | ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l, i, j => by
+    -- Step 1: `⌈log₂ L⌉ = ⌈log₂ ⌈L/2⌉⌉ + 1` for the length `L ≥ 2`.
+    have hlen : 2 ≤ (ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length := by
+      rw [List.length_cons, List.length_cons]
+      omega
+    have hc := Nat.clog_of_two_le (b := 2) (by norm_num) hlen
+    -- Step 2: both halves have length at most `⌈L/2⌉`, so the induction hypothesis bounds them.
+    have hT : ∀ i j, (compTree ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).take ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length / 2)) i j).depth ≤
+        (Nat.clog 2 w + 2) * Nat.clog 2 (((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length + 2 - 1) / 2) + 1 := by
+      intro i j
+      refine (depth_compTree_list_le _ i j).trans (Nat.add_le_add_right
+        (Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ (Nat.clog_mono_right 2 ?_)) 1)
+      rw [List.length_take]
+      omega
+    have hD : ∀ i j, (compTree ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).drop ((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length / 2)) i j).depth ≤
+        (Nat.clog 2 w + 2) * Nat.clog 2 (((ι₁ :: ι₂ :: l).length + 2 - 1) / 2) + 1 := by
+      intro i j
+      refine (depth_compTree_list_le _ i j).trans (Nat.add_le_add_right
+        (Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ (Nat.clog_mono_right 2 ?_)) 1)
+      rw [List.length_drop]
+      omega
+    -- Step 3: one composition circuit adds `⌈log₂ w⌉ + 2` levels.
+    rw [compTree_cons_cons]
+    refine (depth_wireComp_le hT hD i j).trans ?_
+    rw [hc, Nat.mul_add_one]
+    omega
+termination_by l => l.length
+decreasing_by
+  all_goals simp only [List.length_take, List.length_drop, List.length_cons]
+  all_goals omega

+decreasing_by

+      ⟨2 ^ ((Nat.clog 2 w + 2) * (a + k) + topDepth w + 1),

+        ≤ 2 ^ (((P.program n).toFormula (P.accept n)).depth + 1) := by omega
+      _ ≤ 2 ^ (B * (Nat.log 2 n + 1) + 1) := Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by norm_num)
+          (Nat.add_le_add_right (hdepth n) 1)

+          rw [← pow_mul, ← pow_add]; ring_nf

+/-- Bits that are `false` from position `i` on add nothing: for `i ≤ m`,
+`(∀ j, i ≤ j → f j = false) → prefixVal f m = prefixVal f i`.  Glue. -/
+theorem prefixVal_eq_of_false {f : ℕ → Bool} {i m : ℕ} (hle : i ≤ m)
+    (h : ∀ j, i ≤ j → f j = false) : prefixVal f m = prefixVal f i := by

+/-- A common depth bound of the members bounds the maximum depth of a list:
+`(∀ c ∈ cs, c.depth ≤ d) → TreeCircuit.maxDepth cs ≤ d`.  Glue. -/
+theorem TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall {cs : List (TreeCircuit n)} {d : ℕ}
+    (h : ∀ c ∈ cs, c.depth ≤ d) : TreeCircuit.maxDepth cs ≤ d := by
+  induction cs with
+  | nil => rw [TreeCircuit.maxDepth_nil]; exact Nat.zero_le d
+  | cons c cs ih =>
+      rw [TreeCircuit.maxDepth_cons]
+      exact max_le (h c List.mem_cons_self) (ih fun c' hc' => h c' (List.mem_cons_of_mem c hc'))

+/-- The full-adder identity summed over `L` bit positions: if `s j = a j ⊕ b j ⊕ c j` and
+`k (j + 1) = maj (a j, b j, c j)` for `j < L` and `k 0 = false`, then
+`prefixVal a L + prefixVal b L + prefixVal c L = prefixVal s L + prefixVal k (L + 1)`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem prefixVal_fullAdder {a b c s k : ℕ → Bool} {L : ℕ}
+    (hs : ∀ j < L, s j = (a j ^^ b j ^^ c j)) (hk0 : k 0 = false)
+    (hk : ∀ j < L, k (j + 1) = (a j && b j || c j && (a j ^^ b j))) :
+    prefixVal a L + prefixVal b L + prefixVal c L = prefixVal s L + prefixVal k (L + 1) := by
+  induction L with
+  | zero =>
+      simp only [prefixVal_zero, prefixVal_succ, hk0, Bool.toNat_false, Nat.zero_mul]
+  | succ L ih =>
+      have ih' := ih (fun j hj => hs j (by omega)) (fun j hj => hk j (by omega))
+      rw [prefixVal_succ a, prefixVal_succ b, prefixVal_succ c, prefixVal_succ s,
+        prefixVal_succ k (L + 1), hs L (by omega), hk L (by omega)]
+      have hfa : (a L).toNat + (b L).toNat + (c L).toNat =
+          (a L ^^ b L ^^ c L).toNat + 2 * (a L && b L || c L && (a L ^^ b L)).toNat := by
+        cases a L <;> cases b L <;> cases c L <;> rfl
+      have h2 := congrArg (· * 2 ^ L) hfa
+      simp only at h2
+      rw [pow_succ]
+      nlinarith [ih', h2]

+/-- One carry-save layer keeps the total:
+`((csaLayer Us).map (val x)).sum = (Us.map (val x)).sum`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem sum_csaLayer (x : Fin n → Bool) :
+    ∀ Us : List (List (TreeCircuit n)), ((csaLayer Us).map (val x)).sum = (Us.map (val x)).sum
+  | A :: B :: C :: Us => by
+      rw [csaLayer_cons_cons_cons]
+      simp only [List.map_cons, List.sum_cons]
+      have h := csaStep_val x A B C
+      rw [sum_csaLayer x Us]
+      omega
+  | [] | [_] | [_, _] => rfl

+/-- One carry-save layer sends length `k` to `2 (k / 3) + k % 3`:
+`(csaLayer Us).length = 2 * (Us.length / 3) + Us.length % 3`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem length_csaLayer :
+    ∀ Us : List (List (TreeCircuit n)),
+      (csaLayer Us).length = 2 * (Us.length / 3) + Us.length % 3
+  | A :: B :: C :: Us => by
+      rw [csaLayer_cons_cons_cons]
+      simp only [List.length_cons]
+      rw [length_csaLayer Us]
+      omega
+  | [] | [_] | [_, _] => by
+      rw [csaLayer_of_length_le_two (by simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega)]
+      simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil]
+
+/-- `j` layers send length `k` to the `j`-th iterate of `k ↦ 2 (k / 3) + k % 3`:
+`(Nat.repeat csaLayer j Us).length = (fun k => 2 * (k / 3) + k % 3)^[j] Us.length`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem length_repeat_csaLayer (Us : List (List (TreeCircuit n))) :
+    ∀ j : ℕ, (Nat.repeat csaLayer j Us).length = (fun k => 2 * (k / 3) + k % 3)^[j] Us.length
+  | 0 => rfl
+  | j + 1 => by
+      show (csaLayer (Nat.repeat csaLayer j Us)).length = _
+      rw [length_csaLayer, length_repeat_csaLayer Us j, Function.iterate_succ_apply']

+/-- After `csaLevels k` steps of `k ↦ 2 (k / 3) + k % 3` at most two numbers are left:
+`(fun k => 2 * (k / 3) + k % 3)^[csaLevels k] k ≤ 2`.  Glue. -/

+/-- A member of a read-off number `Vs.getD i []` is a member of the flattened list:
+`c ∈ Vs.getD i [] → c ∈ Vs.flatten`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem mem_flatten_of_mem_getD {Vs : List (List (TreeCircuit n))} {i : ℕ}
+    {c : TreeCircuit n} (h : c ∈ Vs.getD i []) : c ∈ Vs.flatten := by
+  by_cases hi : i < Vs.length
+  · rw [List.getD_eq_getElem Vs [] hi] at h
+    exact List.mem_flatten_of_mem (List.getElem_mem hi) h
+  · rw [List.getD_eq_default Vs [] (Nat.le_of_not_lt hi)] at h
+    nomatch h

+/-- One carry-save layer raises a common depth bound `d ≥ 1` of all formulas by at most `4`:
+`1 ≤ d → (∀ c ∈ Us.flatten, c.depth ≤ d) → ∀ c ∈ (csaLayer Us).flatten, c.depth ≤ d + 4`.
+Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Recursion along `csaLayer`'s cases.  For a triple `A, B, C`, the bound gives
+`maxDepth (A ++ B ++ C) ≤ d` (`maxDepth_le_of_forall`), so both words of `csaStep A B C` lie
+within `max 1 d + 4 = d + 4` (`depth_csaStep_le`, `depth_le_maxDepth`); the rest of the layer is
+bounded by the recursive call.  A remainder of at most two numbers is kept unchanged. -/
+private theorem depth_csaLayer_le {d : ℕ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) :
+    ∀ Us : List (List (TreeCircuit n)), (∀ c ∈ Us.flatten, c.depth ≤ d) →
+      ∀ c ∈ (csaLayer Us).flatten, c.depth ≤ d + 4
+  | A :: B :: C :: Us, h => by
+      have hABC : TreeCircuit.maxDepth (A ++ B ++ C) ≤ d :=
+        TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall fun c hc => by
+          refine h c ?_
+          rw [List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, ← List.append_assoc,
+            ← List.append_assoc]
+          exact List.mem_append_left _ hc
+      have hSK := depth_csaStep_le A B C
+      have hUs := depth_csaLayer_le hd Us fun c hc => by
+        refine h c ?_
+        rw [List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons]
+        exact List.mem_append_right _ (List.mem_append_right _ (List.mem_append_right _ hc))
+      intro c hc
+      rw [csaLayer_cons_cons_cons, List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, ← List.append_assoc,
+        List.mem_append] at hc
+      rcases hc with hc | hc
+      · have := TreeCircuit.depth_le_maxDepth hc
+        omega
+      · exact hUs c hc
+  | [], h | [_], h | [_, _], h => fun c hc => (h c hc).trans (Nat.le_add_right d 4)
+
+/-- One carry-save layer keeps a common fan-in bound `d ≥ 2` of all formulas:
+`2 ≤ d → (∀ c ∈ Us.flatten, c.maxFanin ≤ d) → ∀ c ∈ (csaLayer Us).flatten, c.maxFanin ≤ d`.
+Glue.

+private theorem maxFanin_csaLayer_le {d : ℕ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) :

+      ∀ c ∈ (csaLayer Us).flatten, c.maxFanin ≤ d

+          rw [List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, ← List.append_assoc,
+            ← List.append_assoc]
+          exact List.mem_append_left _ hc
+      have hSK := maxFaninL_csaStep_le A B C
+      have hUs := maxFanin_csaLayer_le hd Us fun c hc => by
+        refine h c ?_
+        rw [List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons]
+        exact List.mem_append_right _ (List.mem_append_right _ (List.mem_append_right _ hc))
+      intro c hc
+      rw [csaLayer_cons_cons_cons, List.flatten_cons, List.flatten_cons, ← List.append_assoc,
+        List.mem_append] at hc
+      rcases hc with hc | hc
+      · have := TreeCircuit.maxFanin_le_maxFaninL hc
+        omega
+      · exact hUs c hc
+  | [], h | [_], h | [_, _], h => h

+/-- A bit padded by `const false` evaluates to the bit sequence:
+`(U.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)).eval x = bitAt x U j`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem eval_getD_const (x : Fin n → Bool) (U : List (TreeCircuit n)) (j : ℕ) :
+    (U.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)).eval x = bitAt x U j := by
+  have h := List.getD_map U (TreeCircuit.const false) (n := j) fun c : TreeCircuit n => c.eval x
+  rw [TreeCircuit.const_eval] at h
+  rw [bitAt_eq, h]

+/-- A formula number is the value of its bit sequence over any `m ≥ |U|` bits:
+`U.length ≤ m → val x U = prefixVal (bitAt x U) m`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt (x : Fin n → Bool) {U : List (TreeCircuit n)} {m : ℕ}
+    (hm : U.length ≤ m) : val x U = prefixVal (bitAt x U) m := by
+  calc val x U = prefixVal (bitAt x U) U.length := by
+        rw [val_eq, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, List.length_map]
+        exact prefixVal_congr fun j _ => (bitAt_eq x U j).symm
+    _ = prefixVal (bitAt x U) m :=
+        (prefixVal_eq_of_false hm fun j hj => by
+          rw [bitAt_eq, List.getD_eq_default _ _ (by rw [List.length_map]; exact hj)]).symm

 /-- The carry into bit position `b` of `U + W`, by generate/propagate: with `U_j, W_j` the bits
 padded by `const false`, `carryOut U W b = ⋁_{j < b} ⋀ [U_j ∧ W_j, U_{j+1} ∨ W_{j+1}, …,
 U_{b-1} ∨ W_{b-1}]` (bit `j` generates a carry and every higher bit below `b` transmits it),
@@ -90,21 +109,90 @@ theorem carryOut_eq (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (b : ℕ) :
               TreeCircuit.or2 (U.getD (j + 1 + k) (TreeCircuit.const false))
                 (W.getD (j + 1 + k) (TreeCircuit.const false)))) := rfl
 
+/-- The ripple carry into position `b` is set exactly when some bit `j < b` generates a carry
+and every bit strictly between `j` and `b` transmits it:
+`carryBit A B b = true ↔ ∃ j < b, A j = true ∧ B j = true ∧
+∀ i, j < i → i < b → (A i || B i) = true`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induction on `b` with `carryBit_zero`/`carryBit_succ`.  At `b + 1`, the carry
+is `(A_b ∧ B_b) ∨ ((A_b ⊕ B_b) ∧ carry_b)`, which equals `(A_b ∧ B_b) ∨ ((A_b ∨ B_b) ∧ carry_b)`
+(cases on the Booleans): the first disjunct is the witness `j = b`, the second extends a
+witness `j < b` of the induction hypothesis by the transmitting bit `b`. -/
+private theorem carryBit_eq_true_iff (A B : ℕ → Bool) (b : ℕ) :
+    carryBit A B b = true ↔
+      ∃ j < b, A j = true ∧ B j = true ∧ ∀ i, j < i → i < b → (A i || B i) = true := by
+  induction b with
+  | zero =>
+      rw [carryBit_zero]
+      exact ⟨fun h => absurd h Bool.false_ne_true, fun ⟨j, hj, _⟩ => absurd hj (Nat.not_lt_zero j)⟩
+  | succ b ih =>
+      rw [carryBit_succ]
+      constructor
+      · intro h
+        by_cases hg : A b = true ∧ B b = true
+        · exact ⟨b, Nat.lt_succ_self b, hg.1, hg.2, fun i hi hib => absurd hib (by omega)⟩
+        -- Step 1: no generation at `b`, so `b` transmits and there is a carry into `b`
+        have ht : (A b || B b) = true ∧ carryBit A B b = true := by
+          revert h hg
+          cases A b <;> cases B b <;> cases carryBit A B b <;> decide
+        obtain ⟨j, hj, hAj, hBj, hm⟩ := ih.1 ht.2
+        refine ⟨j, by omega, hAj, hBj, fun i hji hib => ?_⟩
+        rcases Nat.lt_succ_iff_lt_or_eq.1 hib with hib | rfl
+        · exact hm i hji hib
+        · exact ht.1
+      · rintro ⟨j, hj, hAj, hBj, hm⟩
+        rcases Nat.lt_succ_iff_lt_or_eq.1 hj with hj | rfl
+        -- Step 2: a witness below `b` gives a carry into `b`, which bit `b` transmits
+        · have hc := ih.2 ⟨j, hj, hAj, hBj, fun i h1 h2 => hm i h1 (by omega)⟩
+          have hor := hm b hj (Nat.lt_succ_self b)
+          rw [hc]
+          revert hor
+          cases A b <;> cases B b <;> decide
+        · simp only [hAj, hBj, Bool.and_self, Bool.true_or]
+
 /-- **F7, value.** The carry-out formula computes the ripple carry into position `b`:
 `(carryOut U W b).eval x = carryBit (bitAt x U) (bitAt x W) b` (`Adder.lean:377`).  Glue
 (r3 §H5.7, F7).  *Statement change:* r3's sketch writes `carryOut U W` with the position `b`
 free; the position is the explicit third argument.
 
 **Proof sketch.** Induction on `b` for the unrolled carry: `carryBit A B (i + 1) =
-(A_i ∧ B_i) ∨ ((A_i ⊕ B_i) ∧ carryBit A B i)` (reader of `carryBit` missing; see the census)
-equals `(A_i ∧ B_i) ∨ ((A_i ∨ B_i) ∧ carryBit A B i)` by cases, and unrolling gives
-`carryBit A B b = ∃ j < b, A_j ∧ B_j ∧ ∀ k, j < k < b → A_k ∨ B_k`.  On the formula side
-`carryOut_eq`, `balanced_eval`, `eval_node_false`/`eval_node_true`, `and2_eval`, `or2_eval`,
-and each padded bit evaluates to `bitAt x U j` (`bitAt_eq`, `const_eval` for the padding,

+/-- A masked constant has the value `x_i · a` when `a < 2 ^ p`:
+`a < 2 ^ p → val x (maskedConst i a p) = (x i).toNat * a`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The bits evaluate to those of `c = if x i then a else 0` (`maskedConst_eq`,
+`eval_lit`, `Lit.eval_eq`, `const_eval`, `Nat.zero_testBit`); read the value through `val_eq`,
+`bitsVal_eq_prefixVal`, `prefixVal_congr` and `prefixVal_testBit`, and `a % 2 ^ p = a`. -/
+private theorem val_maskedConst (x : Fin n → Bool) (i : Fin n) {a p : ℕ} (ha : a < 2 ^ p) :
+    val x (maskedConst i a p) = (x i).toNat * a := by
+  -- Step 1: the bits are those of `c = if x i then a else 0`
+  have hbits : ((maskedConst i a p).map fun c => c.eval x) =
+      (List.range p).map (if x i = true then a else 0).testBit := by
+    rw [maskedConst_eq, List.map_map]
+    refine List.map_congr_left fun j _ => ?_
+    by_cases hj : a.testBit j
+    · rw [Function.comp_apply, if_pos hj, TreeCircuit.eval_lit, Lit.eval_eq]
+      by_cases hx : x i = true
+      · rw [if_pos rfl, if_pos hx, hx, hj]
+      · rw [if_pos rfl, if_neg hx, Nat.zero_testBit, Bool.eq_false_iff.2 hx]
+    · rw [Function.comp_apply, if_neg hj, TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+      by_cases hx : x i = true
+      · rw [if_pos hx, Bool.eq_false_iff.2 hj]
+      · rw [if_neg hx, Nat.zero_testBit]
+  -- Step 2: read the value through `prefixVal`
+  rw [val_eq, hbits, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, List.length_map, List.length_range]
+  rw [prefixVal_congr (g := (if x i = true then a else 0).testBit) fun j hj => by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_map, List.getElem?_range hj, Option.map_some,
+      Option.getD_some], prefixVal_testBit]
+  by_cases hx : x i = true
+  · rw [if_pos hx, hx, Bool.toNat_true, one_mul, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt ha]
+  · rw [if_neg hx, Bool.eq_false_iff.2 hx, Bool.toNat_false, zero_mul, Nat.zero_mod]
+
+/-- Bits above position `w` of a number below `2 ^ w` are zero:
+`w ≤ m → prefixVal f m < 2 ^ w → prefixVal f m = prefixVal f w`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem prefixVal_eq_of_lt_two_pow {f : ℕ → Bool} {w m : ℕ} (hwm : w ≤ m)
+    (h : prefixVal f m < 2 ^ w) : prefixVal f m = prefixVal f w := by
+  induction m, hwm using Nat.le_induction with
+  | base => rfl
+  | succ m hwm ih =>
+      have h2 : 2 ^ w ≤ 2 ^ m := Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by norm_num) hwm
+      rw [prefixVal_succ] at h ⊢
+      rcases Bool.eq_false_or_eq_true (f m) with hf | hf
+      · rw [hf, Bool.toNat_true, Nat.one_mul] at h
+        omega
+      · rw [hf, Bool.toNat_false, Nat.zero_mul, Nat.add_zero] at h ⊢
+        exact ih h
+
+/-- Trimming a formula number to `w` bits keeps its value when that value is below `2 ^ w`:
+`val x U < 2 ^ w → val x (U.take w) = val x U`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem val_take_of_lt (x : Fin n → Bool) {U : List (TreeCircuit n)} {w : ℕ}
+    (h : val x U < 2 ^ w) : val x (U.take w) = val x U := by
+  rcases le_total U.length w with hU | hU
+  · rw [List.take_of_length_le hU]
+  · rw [val_eq, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal] at h
+    have hU' : w ≤ (U.map fun c => c.eval x).length := by rwa [List.length_map]
+    rw [val_eq, val_eq, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, List.map_take,
+      List.length_take, Nat.min_eq_left hU']
+    refine (prefixVal_congr fun j hj => ?_).trans (prefixVal_eq_of_lt_two_pow hU' h).symm
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_take_of_lt hj]

+/-! ## Correctness of the NAND gadget -/
+
+/-- Glue: over the depth run of `gs`, a gate has one level more than its deepest argument,
+measured by `vertexDepth`:
+`g.depth (runWith DAGGate.depth gs (List.replicate n 0)) =
+1 + (g.args.map (vertexDepth n gs)).foldr max 0`, by `DAGGate.depth_eq` and the reader
+`vertexDepth_eq` (`TreeDAG.lean`) on each argument. -/
+private theorem depth_runWith_eq {g : DAGGate} {gs : List DAGGate} :
+    g.depth (runWith DAGGate.depth gs (List.replicate n 0)) =
+      1 + (g.args.map (vertexDepth n gs)).foldr max 0 := by
+  rw [DAGGate.depth_eq, funext (vertexDepth_eq n gs)]
+
+/-- Glue: a one-gate gadget.  Emitting a single NAND gate `g₁` that reads the arguments of `g`
+and computes `g` meets `GadgetSpec` with depth factor `2`:
+`GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs (gs ++ [g₁], n + gs.length)`.  It
+covers the copied `¬` gate and `∨[] ↦ NAND[]`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The new vertex is the last one, so its value and depth are `g₁`'s over the
+run of `gs` (`vertexValue_last`, `vertexDepth_last`); `depth_runWith_eq` gives one level over
+the deepest argument. -/
+private theorem gadgetSpec_single {g g₁ : DAGGate} {gs : List DAGGate} (hgs : GatesAcyclic n gs)
+    (hargs : ∀ a ∈ g.args, a < n + gs.length) (hg₁ : g₁.IsNAND) (hga : g₁.args = g.args)
+    (hval : ∀ vals, g₁.eval vals = g.eval vals) :
+    GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs (gs ++ [g₁], n + gs.length) := by
+  have hargs₁ : ∀ a ∈ g₁.args, a < n + gs.length := hga ▸ hargs
+  refine ⟨⟨[g₁], rfl⟩, hgs.snoc hargs₁, ?_, fun x => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]; omega
+  · exact (vertexValue_last gs g₁ x).trans (hval _)
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]; omega
+  · show vertexDepth n (gs ++ [g₁]) (n + gs.length) ≤ _
+    rw [vertexDepth_last, depth_runWith_eq, hga]
+    omega
+  · intro h hh
+    simp only [List.drop_left, List.mem_singleton] at hh
+    exact hh ▸ hg₁
+
+/-- Glue: a two-gate gadget.  Emitting a NAND gate `g₁` that reads the arguments of `g` and
+computes `¬g`, then its negation `NAND[g₁]`, meets `GadgetSpec` with depth factor `2`:
+`GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs
+(gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)`.  It covers `∧[]`, `∧[a]`, `∨[a]`
+and `∧[a, b]`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The output vertex is the last one: its value is `¬` of vertex
+`n + |gs|`, which is `g₁`'s value over the run of `gs` (`vertexValue_last`,
+`runWith_getD_last`), hence `g`'s by `hval`.  Its depth is one more than `g₁`'s, which is one
+more than the deepest argument (`depth_runWith_eq`). -/
+private theorem gadgetSpec_double {g g₁ : DAGGate} {gs : List DAGGate} (hgs : GatesAcyclic n gs)
+    (hargs : ∀ a ∈ g.args, a < n + gs.length) (hg₁ : g₁.IsNAND) (hga : g₁.args = g.args)
+    (hval : ∀ vals, (!g₁.eval vals) = g.eval vals) :
+    GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs
+      (gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1) := by
+  have hargs₁ : ∀ a ∈ g₁.args, a < n + gs.length := hga ▸ hargs
+  have e : gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩] = gs ++ [g₁] ++ [⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩] := by
+    rw [List.append_assoc]; rfl
+  have hl₁ : (gs ++ [g₁]).length = gs.length + 1 := by
+    rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]
+  have hm : n + gs.length + 1 = n + (gs ++ [g₁]).length := by rw [hl₁]; omega
+  have h₂ : (⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND :=
+    (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, by simp⟩
+  refine ⟨⟨[g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], rfl⟩, ?_, ?_, fun x => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · rw [e]
+    refine (hgs.snoc hargs₁).snoc fun a ha => ?_
+    rw [List.mem_singleton.mp ha, hl₁]
+    omega
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- value: the output negates vertex `n + |gs|`, which computes `¬g`
+    have hlast := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.eval gs g₁ (List.ofFn x) false
+    rw [List.length_ofFn] at hlast
+    show vertexValue (gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩]) x (n + gs.length + 1) = _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexValue_last, DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND h₂, List.all_cons, List.all_nil,
+      Bool.and_true, hlast, hval]
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- depth: one level over `g₁`, which is one level over the deepest argument
+    have hlast := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.depth gs g₁ (List.replicate n 0) 0
+    rw [List.length_replicate, depth_runWith_eq, hga] at hlast
+    show vertexDepth n (gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩]) (n + gs.length + 1) ≤ _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexDepth_last, DAGGate.depth_eq, List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons,
+      List.foldr_nil, hlast]
+    omega
+  · intro h hh
+    simp only [List.drop_left, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hh
+    rcases hh with rfl | rfl
+    · exact hg₁
+    · exact h₂
+
+/-- Glue: the three-gate gadget of `∨[a, b]`, `NAND[a]`, `NAND[b]`, `NAND[¬a, ¬b]`, meets
+`GadgetSpec` with depth factor `2`:
+`GadgetSpec n 2 4 DAGGate.IsNAND ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs (nandGadget n ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs)`, written
+out by `nandGadget_or_pair`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** De Morgan: the output is `¬(¬a ∧ ¬b) = a ∨ b`.  The two negations sit at
+vertices `n + |gs|` and `n + |gs| + 1` and read only old vertices, whose values and depths
+the later gates do not change (`runWith_append`, `runWith_getD_of_lt`); each new vertex is
+evaluated by `runWith_getD_last` and `DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND`, and its depth by
+`depth_runWith_eq`.  The output is two levels above the deeper of `a`, `b`. -/
+private theorem gadgetSpec_orPair {a b : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} (hgs : GatesAcyclic n gs)
+    (hargs : ∀ v ∈ (⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ : DAGGate).args, v < n + gs.length) :
+    GadgetSpec n 2 ((⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ : DAGGate).args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩],
+        n + gs.length + 2) := by
+  have ha : a < n + gs.length := hargs a (by simp)
+  have hb : b < n + gs.length := hargs b (by simp)
+  have hN : ∀ l : List ℕ, l.length ≤ 2 → (⟨.not, l⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND := fun l hl =>
+    (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, hl⟩
+  -- the three new gates, and the gate lists after one and two of them
+  set g₁ : DAGGate := ⟨.not, [a]⟩ with hg₁
+  set g₂ : DAGGate := ⟨.not, [b]⟩ with hg₂
+  set g₃ : DAGGate := ⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩ with hg₃
+  have e : gs ++ [g₁, g₂, g₃] = gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂] ++ [g₃] := by
+    simp only [List.append_assoc]; rfl
+  have hl₁ : (gs ++ [g₁]).length = gs.length + 1 := by
+    rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]
+  have hl₂ : (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]).length = gs.length + 2 := by
+    rw [List.length_append, hl₁, List.length_singleton]
+  have hm : n + gs.length + 2 = n + (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]).length := by rw [hl₂]; omega
+  refine ⟨⟨[g₁, g₂, g₃], rfl⟩, ?_, ?_, fun x => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · rw [e]
+    refine ((hgs.snoc fun v hv => ?_).snoc fun v hv => ?_).snoc fun v hv => ?_
+    · rw [List.mem_singleton.mp hv]; exact ha
+    · rw [List.mem_singleton.mp hv, hl₁]; omega
+    · simp only [hg₃, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hv
+      rw [hl₂]; omega
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- value: `¬(¬a ∧ ¬b) = a ∨ b`
+    set V := runWith DAGGate.eval gs (List.ofFn x)
+    set V₁ := runWith DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁]) (List.ofFn x) with hV₁
+    have h₁ : V₁.getD (n + gs.length) false = g₁.eval V := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.eval gs g₁ (List.ofFn x) false
+      rwa [List.length_ofFn] at this
+    have h₂ : (runWith DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.ofFn x)).getD
+        (n + gs.length + 1) false = g₂.eval V₁ := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁]) g₂ (List.ofFn x) false
+      rwa [List.length_ofFn, hl₁, ← Nat.add_assoc] at this
+    have h₁₂ : (runWith DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.ofFn x)).getD
+        (n + gs.length) false = V₁.getD (n + gs.length) false := by
+      rw [runWith_append, runWith_getD_of_lt]
+      rw [length_runWith, List.length_ofFn, hl₁]; omega
+    have hb₁ : V₁.getD b false = V.getD b false := by
+      rw [hV₁, runWith_append, runWith_getD_of_lt]
+      rw [length_runWith, List.length_ofFn]; exact hb
+    show vertexValue (gs ++ [g₁, g₂, g₃]) x (n + gs.length + 2) = _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexValue_last, DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (hN [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]
+      (by simp)), List.all_cons, List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true, h₁₂, h₁, h₂, hg₁, hg₂,
+      DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (hN [a] (by simp)), DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (hN [b] (by simp))]
+    simp only [List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true]
+    rw [hb₁, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+    simp only [List.any_cons, List.any_nil, Bool.or_false]
+    cases V.getD a false <;> cases V.getD b false <;> rfl
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- depth: two levels over the deeper of `a` and `b`
+    have hd₁ : (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD (n + gs.length) 0
+        = 1 + max (vertexDepth n gs a) 0 := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.depth gs g₁ (List.replicate n 0) 0
+      rw [List.length_replicate, depth_runWith_eq] at this
+      rw [this, hg₁, List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil]
+    have hd₂ : (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD
+        (n + gs.length + 1) 0 = 1 + max (vertexDepth n gs b) 0 := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁]) g₂ (List.replicate n 0) 0
+      rw [List.length_replicate, hl₁, ← Nat.add_assoc, depth_runWith_eq, hg₂, List.map_cons,
+        List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil, vertexDepth_append gs [g₁] hb] at this
+      exact this
+    have hd₁₂ : (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD
+        (n + gs.length) 0 =
+          (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD (n + gs.length) 0 := by
+      rw [runWith_append, runWith_getD_of_lt]
+      rw [length_runWith, List.length_replicate, hl₁]; omega
+    show vertexDepth n (gs ++ [g₁, g₂, g₃]) (n + gs.length + 2) ≤ _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexDepth_last, DAGGate.depth_eq, hg₃, List.map_cons, List.map_cons,
+      List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil, hd₁₂, hd₁, hd₂]
+    simp only [List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil]
+    omega
+  · intro h hh
+    simp only [List.drop_left, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hh
+    rcases hh with rfl | rfl | rfl
+    · exact hN _ (by simp)
+    · exact hN _ (by simp)
+    · exact hN _ (by simp)

   LWE*, FOCS 2011 (full version).

 theorem DAGCircuit.layerOutput_eq [NeZero n] (C : DAGCircuit n) :

+/-- The chain only extends the gate list: `gs <+: (layerChain N gs a b s).1`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem layerChain_isPrefix {N : ℕ} :
+    ∀ {s : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} {a b : ℕ}, gs <+: (layerChain N gs a b s).1
+  | 0, gs, a, b => by rw [layerChain_zero]
+  | s + 1, gs, a, b => by
+    rw [layerChain_succ]; exact (List.prefix_append _ _).trans layerChain_isPrefix
+
+/-- The chain appends two gates per link:
+`(layerChain N gs a b s).1.length = gs.length + 2 * s`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem length_layerChain_fst {N : ℕ} :
+    ∀ {s : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} {a b : ℕ}, (layerChain N gs a b s).1.length = gs.length + 2 * s
+  | 0, gs, a, b => by rw [layerChain_zero]; rfl
+  | s + 1, gs, a, b => by
+    rw [layerChain_succ, length_layerChain_fst, List.length_append]; simp; omega
+
+/-- Every gate the chain appends reads one vertex: `g ∈ (layerChain N gs a b s).1 →
+g ∈ gs ∨ ∃ w, g = ⟨.not, [w]⟩`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem mem_layerChain_fst {N : ℕ} :
+    ∀ {s : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} {a b : ℕ} {g : DAGGate}, g ∈ (layerChain N gs a b s).1 →
+      g ∈ gs ∨ ∃ w, g = ⟨.not, [w]⟩
+  | 0, gs, a, b, g, hg => by rw [layerChain_zero] at hg; exact Or.inl hg
+  | s + 1, gs, a, b, g, hg => by
+    rw [layerChain_succ] at hg
+    rcases mem_layerChain_fst hg with hg | hg
+    · simp only [List.mem_append, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hg
+      rcases hg with hg | rfl | rfl
+      exacts [Or.inl hg, Or.inr ⟨_, rfl⟩, Or.inr ⟨_, rfl⟩]
+    · exact Or.inr hg
+
+/-- The chain keeps a gate list acyclic, and its column `a :: b :: (outer vertices)` lies below
+the vertex count, when `a` and `b` exist: `GatesAcyclic N gs → a < N + |gs| → b < N + |gs| →
+GatesAcyclic N (layerChain N gs a b s).1 ∧
+∀ w ∈ a :: b :: (layerChain N gs a b s).2, w < N + (layerChain N gs a b s).1.length`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem layerChain_acyclic {N : ℕ} :
+    ∀ {s : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} {a b : ℕ}, GatesAcyclic N gs → a < N + gs.length →
+      b < N + gs.length → GatesAcyclic N (layerChain N gs a b s).1 ∧
+        ∀ w ∈ a :: b :: (layerChain N gs a b s).2, w < N + (layerChain N gs a b s).1.length
+  | 0, gs, a, b, h, ha, hb => by rw [layerChain_zero]; exact ⟨h, by simp [ha, hb]⟩
+  | s + 1, gs, a, b, h, ha, hb => by
+    have h2 := (h.snoc (g := ⟨.not, [a]⟩) (by simpa using ha)).snoc
+      (g := ⟨.not, [N + gs.length]⟩) (by simp)
+    rw [List.append_assoc] at h2
+    have hl := (layerChain_isPrefix (N := N) (s := s + 1) (gs := gs) (a := a) (b := b)).length_le
+    rw [layerChain_succ] at hl ⊢
+    obtain ⟨h3, h4⟩ := layerChain_acyclic (s := s) (a := b) (b := N + gs.length + 1) h2
+      (by simp; omega) (by simp; omega)
+    refine ⟨h3, fun w hw => ?_⟩
+    rcases List.mem_cons.mp hw with rfl | hw
+    exacts [by omega, h4 w hw]
+
+/-- A recorded copy is below any positive bound `B` of all column entries:
+`0 < B → (∀ col ∈ cols, ∀ w ∈ col, w < B) → C.layerAt cols v k < B` (a missing copy reads as
+`0`).  Glue. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerAt_lt (C : DAGCircuit n) {cols : List (List ℕ)} {B : ℕ}
+    (hB : 0 < B) (h : ∀ col ∈ cols, ∀ w ∈ col, w < B) (v k : ℕ) : C.layerAt cols v k < B := by
+  rw [layerAt_eq, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD]
+  rcases hc : cols[v]? with _ | col
+  · simpa using hB
+  · rcases hw : col[k - C.depthAt v]? with _ | w
+    · simpa [hw] using hB
+    · simpa [hw] using h col (List.mem_of_getElem? hc) w (List.mem_of_getElem? hw)
+
+/-- One fold step, structurally: if the gates `s.1` are acyclic over the `n + n` literal inputs
+and every column entry of `s.2` lies below `n + n + |s.1|`, then `C.layerColumn s v` appends one
+column with entries below its new vertex count, extends `s.1` (`s.1 <+: _`) acyclically by at
+most `2 * C.depth + 1` gates, and keeps every gate a NAND gate when `C.IsNANDForm`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Case split along `layerColumn_eq`.  Step 1: a column `a :: b :: chain` after
+`p` new gates `pre` (`layerChain_acyclic`, `length_layerChain_fst`, `mem_layerChain_fst`).
+A dead vertex and an input with `D = 0` add no gate; an input adds the twin `NAND[n + v]`; a
+gate adds the base, reading recorded copies (`layerAt_lt`), and, below the top, the twin, reading
+copies or `NAND[0, n]` then `NAND[m + 1]`.  The new gates are NAND gates by `isNAND_iff` (the
+copies keep the old fan-in; the old gate is a NAND gate or the default `⟨.not, []⟩`). -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerColumn_struct [NeZero n] (C : DAGCircuit n)
+    (s : List DAGGate × List (List ℕ)) (v : ℕ) (hacy : GatesAcyclic (n + n) s.1)
+    (hcol : ∀ col ∈ s.2, ∀ w ∈ col, w < n + n + s.1.length)
+    (hnand : C.IsNANDForm → ∀ g ∈ s.1, g.IsNAND) :
+    (∃ col, (C.layerColumn s v).2 = s.2 ++ [col] ∧
+      ∀ w ∈ col, w < n + n + (C.layerColumn s v).1.length) ∧
+    s.1 <+: (C.layerColumn s v).1 ∧ GatesAcyclic (n + n) (C.layerColumn s v).1 ∧
+    (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ g ∈ (C.layerColumn s v).1, g.IsNAND) ∧
+    (C.layerColumn s v).1.length ≤ s.1.length + (2 * C.depth + 1) := by
+  have hn : 0 < n := Nat.pos_of_neZero n
+  have h1 : ∀ w : ℕ, (⟨.not, [w]⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND := fun w =>
+    (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, by simp⟩
+  -- Step 1: a column `a :: b :: chain` after the `p` gates `pre`
+  have hch : ∀ (pre : List DAGGate) (p a b t : ℕ), pre.length = p →
+      GatesAcyclic (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) → (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ g ∈ pre, g.IsNAND) →
+      a < n + n + s.1.length + p → b < n + n + s.1.length + p → p + 2 * t ≤ 2 * C.depth + 1 →
+      (∃ col, s.2 ++ [a :: b :: (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).2] = s.2 ++ [col] ∧
+        ∀ w ∈ col, w < n + n + (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1.length) ∧
+      s.1 <+: (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1 ∧
+      GatesAcyclic (n + n) (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1 ∧
+      (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ g ∈ (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1, g.IsNAND) ∧
+      (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1.length ≤ s.1.length + (2 * C.depth + 1) := by
+    intro pre p a b t hp hacy' hpre ha hb ht
+    obtain ⟨h3, h4⟩ := layerChain_acyclic (s := t) (a := a) (b := b) hacy'
+      (by simp; omega) (by simp; omega)
+    refine ⟨⟨_, rfl, h4⟩, (List.prefix_append _ _).trans layerChain_isPrefix, h3,
+      fun hC g hg => ?_, by rw [length_layerChain_fst, List.length_append]; omega⟩
+    rcases mem_layerChain_fst hg with hg | ⟨w, rfl⟩
+    · rcases List.mem_append.mp hg with hg | hg
+      exacts [hnand hC g hg, hpre hC g hg]
+    · exact h1 w
+  rw [layerColumn_eq]
+  by_cases hd : C.depth < C.depthAt v
+  · rw [if_pos hd]; exact ⟨⟨[], rfl, by simp⟩, List.prefix_refl _, hacy, hnand, by simp⟩
+  rw [if_neg hd]
+  by_cases hv : v < n
+  · rw [if_pos hv]
+    by_cases h3 : C.depth = 0
+    · rw [if_pos h3]
+      exact ⟨⟨[v], rfl, by simp; omega⟩, List.prefix_refl _, hacy, hnand, by simp⟩
+    rw [if_neg h3]
+    exact hch _ 1 _ _ _ rfl (hacy.snoc (by simp; omega))
+      (fun _ g hg => by rw [List.mem_singleton.mp hg]; exact h1 _) (by omega) (by omega)
+      (by omega)
+  rw [if_neg hv]
+  have hgN : C.IsNANDForm → (C.gates.getD (v - n) ⟨.not, []⟩).IsNAND := by
+    intro hC
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD]
+    rcases hg : C.gates[v - n]? with _ | g
+    · exact (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, by simp⟩
+    · exact (DAGCircuit.isNANDForm_iff C).mp hC g (List.mem_of_getElem? hg)
+  generalize C.gates.getD (v - n) ⟨.not, []⟩ = g at hgN ⊢
+  -- the base and the twin read recorded copies, below the vertex count, and keep the fan-in
+  have hb : ∀ k, (∀ w ∈ (⟨.not, g.args.map fun a => C.layerAt s.2 a k⟩ : DAGGate).args,
+      w < n + n + s.1.length) ∧
+        (C.IsNANDForm → (⟨.not, g.args.map fun a => C.layerAt s.2 a k⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND) :=
+    fun k => ⟨by
+      simp only [List.mem_map]; rintro _ ⟨a, -, rfl⟩; exact C.layerAt_lt (by omega) hcol a k,
+      fun hC => (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr
+        ⟨rfl, by simpa using ((DAGGate.isNAND_iff g).mp (hgN hC)).2⟩⟩
+  have hacy1 := hacy.snoc (hb (C.depthAt v - 1)).1
+  by_cases h4 : C.depth = C.depthAt v
+  · rw [if_pos h4]
+    refine ⟨⟨_, rfl, by simp⟩, List.prefix_append _ _, hacy1, fun hC g' hg' => ?_, by simp⟩
+    rcases List.mem_append.mp hg' with hg' | hg'
+    exacts [hnand hC g' hg', (List.mem_singleton.mp hg') ▸ (hb _).2 hC]
+  rw [if_neg h4]
+  dsimp only
+  by_cases h5 : g.args = []
+  · rw [if_pos h5]
+    dsimp only
+    rw [List.append_assoc]
+    have h2 := (hacy1.snoc (g := ⟨.not, [0, n]⟩) (by simp; omega)).snoc
+      (g := ⟨.not, [n + n + s.1.length + 1]⟩) (by simp; omega)
+    exact hch _ 3 _ _ _ rfl (by simpa using h2) (fun hC g' hg' => by
+        simp only [List.mem_append, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hg'
+        rcases hg' with rfl | rfl | rfl
+        exacts [(hb _).2 hC, (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, by simp⟩, h1 _])
+      (by omega) (by omega) (by omega)
+  · rw [if_neg h5]
+    dsimp only
+    rw [List.append_assoc]
+    have h2 := hacy1.snoc (g := ⟨.not, g.args.map fun a => C.layerAt s.2 a (C.depthAt v)⟩)
+      (fun w hw => by have := (hb _).1 w hw; simp; omega)
+    exact hch _ 2 _ _ _ rfl (by simpa using h2) (fun hC g' hg' => by
+        simp only [List.mem_append, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hg'
+        rcases hg' with rfl | rfl
+        exacts [(hb _).2 hC, (hb _).2 hC])
+      (by omega) (by omega) (by omega)
+
+/-- The column fold, structurally, after the old vertices `0, …, v - 1`: with
+`s = (List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])`, `s.2.length = v`, `s.1` is acyclic over the
+`n + n` inputs, every column entry lies below `n + n + |s.1|`, every gate is a NAND gate when
+`C.IsNANDForm`, and `s.1.length ≤ (2 * C.depth + 1) * v`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induction on `v` with `List.range_succ` and `List.foldl_append`: one step is
+`layerColumn_struct`, whose hypotheses are the induction hypothesis, and whose conclusions give
+the five parts for `v + 1` (the length bound adds at most `2 * C.depth + 1` gates). -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerFold_struct [NeZero n] (C : DAGCircuit n) (v : ℕ) :
+    ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).2.length = v ∧
+    GatesAcyclic (n + n) ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).1 ∧
+    (∀ col ∈ ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).2, ∀ w ∈ col,
+      w < n + n + ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).1.length) ∧
+    (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ g ∈ ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).1, g.IsNAND) ∧
+    ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).1.length ≤ (2 * C.depth + 1) * v := by
+  induction v with
+  | zero => exact ⟨rfl, GatesAcyclic.nil, by simp, by simp, by simp⟩
+  | succ v ih =>
+    rw [List.range_succ, List.foldl_append, List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_nil]
+    generalize (List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], []) = s at ih ⊢
+    obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, h5⟩ := ih
+    obtain ⟨⟨col, hc, hcw⟩, hp, ha, hn, hl⟩ := C.layerColumn_struct s v h2 h3 h4
+    refine ⟨by rw [hc, List.length_append, h1]; rfl, ha, fun c hc' w hw => ?_, hn,
+      by rw [Nat.mul_succ]; omega⟩
+    rw [hc, List.mem_append, List.mem_singleton] at hc'
+    rcases hc' with hc' | rfl
+    · have := h3 c hc' w hw; have := hp.length_le; omega
+    · exact hcw w hw
+

+/-- Under `C.IsNANDForm`, every gate of the layering is a NAND gate:
+`C.IsNANDForm → ∀ g ∈ C.layerGates, g.IsNAND`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerGates_isNAND (hC : C.IsNANDForm) :
+    ∀ g ∈ C.layerGates, g.IsNAND := by
+  have h := (C.layerFold_struct (n + C.gates.length)).2.2.2.1 hC

+/-- A list of naturals all of whose entries equal `k`, with an entry `y₀`, has `foldr max 0`
+equal to `k`: `(∀ y ∈ l, y = k) → y₀ ∈ l → l.foldr max 0 = k`, from Mathlib's
+`List.max_le_of_forall_le` and `List.le_max_of_le` (`⊥ = 0` on `ℕ`).  Glue. -/
+private theorem foldr_max_eq_of_forall {l : List ℕ} {k y₀ : ℕ} (h : ∀ y ∈ l, y = k)
+    (h₀ : y₀ ∈ l) : l.foldr max 0 = k := by
+  rw [← Nat.bot_eq_zero]
+  exact le_antisymm (List.max_le_of_forall_le l k fun y hy => (h y hy).le)
+    (List.le_max_of_le h₀ (h y₀ h₀).ge)

+/-- The depth of a gate `g` of the layering, read off a prefix `gs ++ [g]` of its gate list:
+`gs ++ [g] <+: C.layerNAND.gates → C.layerNAND.depthAt (n + n + gs.length) =
+1 + (g.args.map C.layerNAND.depthAt).foldr max 0`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerNAND_depthAt_snoc {gs : List DAGGate} {g : DAGGate}

+      1 + (g.args.map C.layerNAND.depthAt).foldr max 0 := by

+  have hd : (fun a => C.layerNAND.depths.getD a 0) = C.layerNAND.depthAt :=
+    funext fun a => (C.layerNAND.depthAt_eq a).symm
+  rw [C.layerNAND.depthAt_gate (lt_of_lt_of_le hi h.length_le), DAGGate.depth_eq,
+    ← h.getElem hi, List.getElem_concat_length rfl, hd]

+/-- The value of a gate `g` of the layering, read off a prefix `gs ++ [g]` of its gate list, when
+`C.IsNANDForm`: `(C.layerNAND.values y).getD (n + n + gs.length) false =

+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerNAND_values_snoc (hC : C.IsNANDForm) {gs : List DAGGate}

+  have hmem : g ∈ C.layerGates := by rw [← layerNAND_gates]; exact h.subset (by simp)
+  rw [C.layerNAND.values_getD_gate y (lt_of_lt_of_le hi h.length_le), ← h.getElem hi,
+    List.getElem_concat_length rfl, DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (C.layerGates_isNAND hC g hmem)]
+
+/-- A one-argument gate `NAND[w]` of the layering, read off a prefix `gs ++ [NAND[w]]`:
+`C.layerNAND.depthAt (n + n + gs.length) = C.layerNAND.depthAt w + 1` and, when `C.IsNANDForm`,
+its value is `!(C.layerNAND.values y).getD w false`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerNAND_single {gs : List DAGGate} {w : ℕ}
+    (h : gs ++ [⟨.not, [w]⟩] <+: C.layerNAND.gates) :
+    C.layerNAND.depthAt (n + n + gs.length) = C.layerNAND.depthAt w + 1 ∧
+      (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ y, (C.layerNAND.values y).getD (n + n + gs.length) false =
+        !(C.layerNAND.values y).getD w false) := by
+  refine ⟨?_, fun hC y => ?_⟩
+  · rw [C.layerNAND_depthAt_snoc h]; simp; omega
+  · rw [C.layerNAND_values_snoc hC h]; simp
+
+/-- The literal inputs of the layering, read as old values: for `u < n`, input vertex `u` holds
+`(C.values x).getD u false` and input vertex `n + u` its negation, on `litInput x`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerNAND_values_lit {u : ℕ} (hu : u < n) (x : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD u false = (C.values x).getD u false ∧
+    (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD (n + u) false = !(C.values x).getD u false := by
+  have h0 := C.values_getD_input x ⟨u, hu⟩
+  have h1 := C.layerNAND.values_getD_input (litInput x) (Fin.castAdd n ⟨u, hu⟩)
+  have h2 := C.layerNAND.values_getD_input (litInput x) (Fin.natAdd n ⟨u, hu⟩)
+  rw [litInput_castAdd] at h1
+  rw [litInput_natAdd] at h2
+  exact ⟨h1.trans h0.symm, h2.trans (congrArg (!·) h0.symm)⟩
+
+/-- The double-NAND chain realizes a column: for a vertex predicate `P` (vertex, level) that
+every link `NAND(NAND(a))` (two consecutive gates of `G` after `gs`) lifts two levels, if the
+chain's gates are a prefix of `G`, `P a L` and `P b (L + 1)`, then
+`P ((a :: b :: (layerChain N gs a b s).2).getD j 0) (L + j)` for every `j ≤ s + 1`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem layerChain_col {G : List DAGGate} {N : ℕ} {P : ℕ → ℕ → Prop}
+    (hstep : ∀ (gs : List DAGGate) (a L : ℕ),
+      gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [N + gs.length]⟩] <+: G → P a L →
+        P (N + gs.length + 1) (L + 2)) :
+    ∀ {s : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} {a b L : ℕ}, (layerChain N gs a b s).1 <+: G → P a L →
+      P b (L + 1) → ∀ j ≤ s + 1, P ((a :: b :: (layerChain N gs a b s).2).getD j 0) (L + j)
+  | 0, gs, a, b, L, _, ha, hb, j, hj => by
+    rw [layerChain_zero]
+    rcases j with _ | _ | j
+    exacts [ha, hb, by omega]
+  | s + 1, gs, a, b, L, hpre, ha, hb, j, hj => by
+    rw [layerChain_succ] at hpre ⊢
+    have key := layerChain_col hstep hpre hb (hstep gs a L (layerChain_isPrefix.trans hpre) ha)
+    rcases j with _ | j
+    · exact ha
+    · rw [List.getD_cons_succ, show L + (j + 1) = L + 1 + j by omega]
+      exact key j (by omega)
+
+/-- One link of the chain over the layering keeps the column invariant of an old vertex `u`:
+if `gs ++ [NAND[a], NAND[n + n + |gs|]]` is a prefix of the layering's gates, then
+`C.layerNAND.depthAt a = L → C.layerNAND.depthAt (n + n + gs.length + 1) = L + 2`, and the outer
+vertex keeps the value of `u` on every literal input when `a` has it (`C.IsNANDForm`).  Glue. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerNAND_link (u : ℕ) {gs : List DAGGate} {a L : ℕ}
+    (hp : gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + n + gs.length]⟩] <+: C.layerNAND.gates)
+    (ha : C.layerNAND.depthAt a = L ∧ (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x,
+      (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD a false = (C.values x).getD u false)) :
+    C.layerNAND.depthAt (n + n + gs.length + 1) = L + 2 ∧ (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x,
+      (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD (n + n + gs.length + 1) false =
+        (C.values x).getD u false) := by
+  have hp2 : gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩] ++ [⟨.not, [n + n + gs.length]⟩] <+: C.layerNAND.gates := by
+    simpa using hp
+  obtain ⟨d1, v1⟩ := C.layerNAND_single ((List.prefix_append _ _).trans hp2)
+  obtain ⟨d2, v2⟩ := C.layerNAND_single hp2
+  have hl : n + n + (gs ++ [(⟨.not, [a]⟩ : DAGGate)]).length = n + n + gs.length + 1 := by
+    simp; omega
+  rw [hl] at d2 v2
+  exact ⟨by omega, fun hC x => by rw [v2 hC, v1 hC, Bool.not_not]; exact ha.2 hC x⟩
+
+/-- One fold step, semantically, over the layering `C.layerNAND` (whose gates extend those of
+the step, `hpre`): if every recorded copy `u@k` of an earlier live vertex `u < v` has depth `k`
+and, under `C.IsNANDForm`, the value of `u` on every literal input, then so has every copy
+`v@k` of the new column, for `C.depthAt v ≤ k ≤ C.depth`; and every new gate reads vertices of
+one common depth.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Case split along `layerColumn_eq`, the copies of `v` read off with
+`layerAt_eq` and `List.getD_append_right`.  Step 1: a column `a :: b :: chain` after new gates
+`pre`, by `layerChain_col` with `layerNAND_link`.  Step 2: an input `v` (`depthAt_input`) is
+`v@0`, and its twin `NAND[n + v]` has depth `1` and value `!!xᵥ` (`layerNAND_single`,
+`layerNAND_values_lit`).  Step 3: a gate `v = n + j` of depth `δ` reads earlier, shallower
+vertices (`depthAt_gate`, `DAGGate.depth_eq`, `C.args_lt`, `DAGGate.getD_lt_depth`) and is a
+NAND of their values (`values_getD_gate`, `DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND`).  Step 4: a NAND gate over
+copies at one level `k` has depth `k + 1` (`foldr_max_eq_of_forall`) and the gate's value
+(`all_congr_mem`); this gives the base `v@δ` (level `δ - 1`; a fan-in-0 base has depth
+`1 = δ`) and the twin (level `δ`).  Step 5: a fan-in-0 twin `NAND(NAND(x₀, ¬x₀))` has depth `2`
+and value `false`. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerColumn_sem (s : List DAGGate × List (List ℕ)) (v : ℕ)
+    (hv : v < n + C.gates.length) (hlen : s.2.length = v)
+    (hpre : (C.layerColumn s v).1 <+: C.layerNAND.gates)
+    (hsem : ∀ u < v, C.depthAt u ≤ C.depth → ∀ k, C.depthAt u ≤ k → k ≤ C.depth →
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt (C.layerAt s.2 u k) = k ∧ (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x,
+        (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD (C.layerAt s.2 u k) false =
+          (C.values x).getD u false)) :
+    (C.depthAt v ≤ C.depth → ∀ k, C.depthAt v ≤ k → k ≤ C.depth →
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt (C.layerAt (C.layerColumn s v).2 v k) = k ∧ (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x,
+        (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD (C.layerAt (C.layerColumn s v).2 v k) false =
+          (C.values x).getD v false)) ∧
+    ∀ g ∈ (C.layerColumn s v).1, g ∈ s.1 ∨ ∃ k, ∀ a ∈ g.args, C.layerNAND.depthAt a = k := by
+  have hn : 0 < n := Nat.pos_of_neZero n
+  let P : ℕ → ℕ → Prop := fun w k => C.layerNAND.depthAt w = k ∧ (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x,
+    (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD w false = (C.values x).getD v false)
+  have hcol : ∀ (col : List ℕ) (k : ℕ),
+      C.layerAt (s.2 ++ [col]) v k = col.getD (k - C.depthAt v) 0 := fun col k => by
+    rw [layerAt_eq, List.getD_append_right _ _ _ _ hlen.le, hlen, Nat.sub_self,
+      List.getD_cons_zero]
+  have hU : ∀ w : ℕ, ∃ k, ∀ a ∈ (⟨.not, [w]⟩ : DAGGate).args, C.layerNAND.depthAt a = k :=
+    fun w => ⟨_, fun a ha => by rw [List.mem_singleton.mp ha]⟩
+  -- Step 1: a column `a :: b :: chain` after the new gates `pre`
+  have hfin : ∀ (pre : List DAGGate) (a b t : ℕ), (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1 <+:
+      C.layerNAND.gates → P a (C.depthAt v) → P b (C.depthAt v + 1) →
+      (∀ g ∈ pre, ∃ k, ∀ a ∈ g.args, C.layerNAND.depthAt a = k) →
+      C.depth = C.depthAt v + t + 1 →
+      (C.depthAt v ≤ C.depth → ∀ k, C.depthAt v ≤ k → k ≤ C.depth →
+        P (C.layerAt (s.2 ++ [a :: b :: (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).2]) v k) k) ∧
+      ∀ g ∈ (layerChain (n + n) (s.1 ++ pre) a b t).1, g ∈ s.1 ∨
+        ∃ k, ∀ a ∈ g.args, C.layerNAND.depthAt a = k := by
+    intro pre a b t hp ha hb hpre ht
+    refine ⟨fun _ k hk1 hk2 => ?_, fun g hg => ?_⟩
+    · rw [hcol]
+      have := layerChain_col (P := P) (fun _ _ _ hp ha => C.layerNAND_link v hp ha) hp ha hb
+        (k - C.depthAt v) (by omega)
+      rwa [Nat.add_sub_of_le hk1] at this
+    · rcases mem_layerChain_fst hg with hg | ⟨w, rfl⟩
+      · rcases List.mem_append.mp hg with hg | hg
+        exacts [Or.inl hg, Or.inr (hpre g hg)]
+      · exact Or.inr (hU w)
+  rw [layerColumn_eq] at hpre ⊢
+  by_cases h1 : C.depth < C.depthAt v
+  · rw [if_pos h1]
+    exact ⟨fun h => absurd h (by omega), fun g hg => Or.inl hg⟩
+  rw [if_neg h1] at hpre ⊢
+  by_cases h2 : v < n
+  · -- Step 2: an input vertex
+    rw [if_pos h2] at hpre ⊢
+    have hv0 : P v (C.depthAt v) :=
+      ⟨(by rw [C.depthAt_input h2]; exact C.layerNAND.depthAt_input (by omega)),
+        fun _ x => (C.layerNAND_values_lit h2 x).1⟩
+    by_cases h3 : C.depth = 0
+    · rw [if_pos h3]
+      refine ⟨fun _ k hk1 hk2 => ?_, fun g hg => Or.inl hg⟩
+      rw [hcol, show k - C.depthAt v = 0 by omega, List.getD_cons_zero,
+        show k = C.depthAt v by omega]
+      exact hv0
+    rw [if_neg h3] at hpre ⊢
+    obtain ⟨d1, v1⟩ := C.layerNAND_single (layerChain_isPrefix.trans hpre)
+    exact hfin _ _ _ _ hpre hv0 ⟨by rw [d1, C.layerNAND.depthAt_input (by omega),
+      C.depthAt_input h2], fun hC x => by
+        rw [v1 hC, (C.layerNAND_values_lit h2 x).2, Bool.not_not]⟩
+      (fun g hg => by rw [List.mem_singleton.mp hg]; exact hU _)
+      (by rw [C.depthAt_input h2]; omega)
+  -- Step 3: a gate vertex `v = n + j`: its depth, arguments and value
+  rw [if_neg h2] at hpre ⊢
+  obtain ⟨j, rfl⟩ : ∃ j, v = n + j := ⟨v - n, by omega⟩
+  have hj : j < C.gates.length := by omega
+  rw [show n + j - n = j by omega, List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ hj] at hpre ⊢
+  have hδ : C.depthAt (n + j) =
+      1 + ((C.gates[j]).args.map fun a => C.depths.getD a 0).foldr max 0 := by
+    rw [C.depthAt_gate hj, DAGGate.depth_eq]
+  have harg : ∀ a ∈ (C.gates[j]).args, a < n + j ∧ C.depthAt a < C.depthAt (n + j) :=
+    fun a ha => ⟨C.args_lt j hj a ha, by
+      rw [C.depthAt_eq a, C.depthAt_gate hj]; exact DAGGate.getD_lt_depth _ _ ha⟩
+  have hval : C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x, (C.values x).getD (n + j) false =
+      !((C.gates[j]).args.all fun a => (C.values x).getD a false) := fun hC x => by
+    rw [C.values_getD_gate x hj,
+      DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND ((C.isNANDForm_iff).mp hC _ (List.getElem_mem hj))]
+  generalize C.gates[j] = g at hδ harg hval hpre ⊢
+  -- Step 4: a NAND gate over the copies at one level `k` of the arguments
+  have hlev := fun k (hk : ∀ a ∈ g.args, C.depthAt a ≤ k) (hkD : k ≤ C.depth) a
+    (ha : a ∈ g.args) => hsem a (harg a ha).1 (le_trans (hk a ha) hkD) k (hk a ha) hkD
+  have hgate : ∀ (gs : List DAGGate) (k : ℕ),
+      gs ++ [⟨.not, g.args.map fun a => C.layerAt s.2 a k⟩] <+: C.layerNAND.gates →
+      g.args ≠ [] → (∀ a ∈ g.args, C.depthAt a ≤ k) → k ≤ C.depth →
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt (n + n + gs.length) = k + 1 ∧ (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x,
+        (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD (n + n + gs.length) false =
+          (C.values x).getD (n + j) false) := by
+    intro gs k hp hne hk hkD
+    refine ⟨?_, fun hC x => ?_⟩
+    · obtain ⟨a₀, ha₀⟩ := List.exists_mem_of_ne_nil _ hne
+      rw [C.layerNAND_depthAt_snoc hp, List.map_map,
+        foldr_max_eq_of_forall (k := k) (fun y hy => ?_) (List.mem_map_of_mem ha₀)]
+      · omega
+      · obtain ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hy
+        exact (hlev k hk hkD a ha).1
+    · rw [C.layerNAND_values_snoc hC hp, List.all_map, hval hC x]
+      exact congrArg (!·) (all_congr_mem fun a ha => (hlev k hk hkD a ha).2 hC x)
+  have hbu : ∀ k, (∀ a ∈ g.args, C.depthAt a ≤ k) → k ≤ C.depth →
+      ∃ k', ∀ b ∈ (⟨.not, g.args.map fun a => C.layerAt s.2 a k⟩ : DAGGate).args,
+        C.layerNAND.depthAt b = k' := fun k hk hkD => ⟨k, fun b hb => by
+    obtain ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hb
+    exact (hlev k hk hkD a ha).1⟩
+  have hδ1 : 1 ≤ C.depthAt (n + j) := by omega
+  have hk1 : ∀ a ∈ g.args, C.depthAt a ≤ C.depthAt (n + j) - 1 := fun a ha => by
+    have := (harg a ha).2; omega
+  set b0 : DAGGate := ⟨.not, g.args.map fun a => C.layerAt s.2 a (C.depthAt (n + j) - 1)⟩
+    with hb0
+  have hbase : s.1 ++ [b0] <+: C.layerNAND.gates → C.depthAt (n + j) ≤ C.depth →
+      P (n + n + s.1.length) (C.depthAt (n + j)) := by
+    intro hp hD
+    by_cases h5 : g.args = []
+    · refine ⟨?_, fun hC x => ?_⟩
+      · rw [C.layerNAND_depthAt_snoc hp, hδ, hb0, h5]; simp
+      · rw [C.layerNAND_values_snoc hC hp, hval hC x, hb0, h5]; simp
+    · have := hgate s.1 _ hp h5 hk1 (by omega)
+      rwa [Nat.sub_add_cancel hδ1] at this
+  by_cases h4 : C.depth = C.depthAt (n + j)
+  · rw [if_pos h4] at hpre ⊢
+    refine ⟨fun _ k hk1 hk2 => ?_, fun g' hg' => ?_⟩
+    · rw [hcol, show k - C.depthAt (n + j) = 0 by omega, List.getD_cons_zero,
+        show k = C.depthAt (n + j) by omega]
+      exact hbase hpre (by omega)
+    · rcases List.mem_append.mp hg' with hg' | hg'
+      exacts [Or.inl hg', Or.inr ((List.mem_singleton.mp hg') ▸ hbu _ hk1 (by omega))]
+  -- Step 5: the parity twin `v@(δ + 1)`, then the chain
+  rw [if_neg h4] at hpre ⊢
+  dsimp only at hpre ⊢
+  by_cases h5 : g.args = []
+  · rw [if_pos h5] at hpre ⊢
+    dsimp only at hpre ⊢
+    have hp' : s.1 ++ [b0] ++ [⟨.not, [0, n]⟩] ++ [⟨.not, [n + n + s.1.length + 1]⟩] <+:
+        C.layerNAND.gates := by rw [List.append_assoc _ [_]]; exact layerChain_isPrefix.trans hpre
+    have hp1 := (List.prefix_append _ _).trans hp'
+    have e1 : n + n + (s.1 ++ [b0]).length = n + n + s.1.length + 1 := by simp; omega
+    have e2 : n + n + (s.1 ++ [b0] ++ [(⟨.not, [0, n]⟩ : DAGGate)]).length =
+        n + n + s.1.length + 2 := by simp; omega
+    have d1 := C.layerNAND_depthAt_snoc hp1
+    obtain ⟨d2, v2⟩ := C.layerNAND_single hp'
+    rw [e1] at d1
+    rw [e2] at d2 v2
+    simp only [List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil, Nat.max_zero,
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt_input (show 0 < n + n by omega),
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt_input (show n < n + n by omega)] at d1
+    have ht : P (n + n + s.1.length + 2) (C.depthAt (n + j) + 1) := by
+      refine ⟨by rw [hδ, h5]; simp; omega, fun hC x => ?_⟩
+      have v1 := C.layerNAND_values_snoc hC hp1 (litInput x)
+      have hl := C.layerNAND_values_lit hn x
+      rw [Nat.add_zero] at hl
+      rw [e1] at v1
+      simp only [List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true, hl.1, hl.2] at v1
+      rw [v2 hC, v1, hval hC x, h5]
+      simp
+    rw [List.append_assoc] at hpre ⊢
+    exact hfin _ _ _ _ hpre (hbase ((List.prefix_append _ _).trans hp1) (by omega)) ht
+      (fun g' hg' => by
+        simp only [List.mem_append, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hg'
+        rcases hg' with rfl | rfl | rfl
+        exacts [hbu _ hk1 (by omega), ⟨0, fun a ha => by
+          simp only [List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at ha
+          rcases ha with rfl | rfl <;> exact C.layerNAND.depthAt_input (by omega)⟩, hU _])
+      (by omega)
+  · rw [if_neg h5] at hpre ⊢
+    dsimp only at hpre ⊢
+    have hp' := layerChain_isPrefix.trans hpre
+    have ht := hgate _ (C.depthAt (n + j)) hp' h5 (fun a ha => (harg a ha).2.le) (by omega)
+    rw [show (s.1 ++ [b0]).length = s.1.length + 1 by simp, ← add_assoc] at ht
+    rw [List.append_assoc] at hpre ⊢
+    exact hfin _ _ _ _ hpre (hbase ((List.prefix_append _ _).trans hp') (by omega)) ht
+      (fun g' hg' => by
+        simp only [List.mem_append, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hg'
+        rcases hg' with rfl | rfl
+        exacts [hbu _ hk1 (by omega), hbu _ (fun a ha => (harg a ha).2.le) (by omega)])
+      (by omega)
+
+/-- The column fold, semantically, over the layering `C.layerNAND`: for
+`s = (List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])` with `v ≤ n + |C.gates|` and `s.1` a prefix
+of the layering's gates, every copy `u@k` (`u < v`, `C.depthAt u ≤ k ≤ C.depth`) has depth `k`
+and, under `C.IsNANDForm`, the value of `u` on every literal input; and every gate of `s.1`
+reads vertices of one common depth.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induction on `v` with `List.range_succ`: `layerColumn_struct` for the shape of a
+step, the induction hypothesis on the prefix, `List.getD_append` for the earlier copies, and
+`layerColumn_sem` for the new column. -/
+private theorem DAGCircuit.layerFold_sem (v : ℕ) (hv : v ≤ n + C.gates.length)
+    (hpre : ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).1 <+: C.layerNAND.gates) :
+    (∀ u < v, C.depthAt u ≤ C.depth → ∀ k, C.depthAt u ≤ k → k ≤ C.depth →
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt (C.layerAt ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).2 u k) = k ∧
+      (C.IsNANDForm → ∀ x, (C.layerNAND.values (litInput x)).getD
+        (C.layerAt ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).2 u k) false =
+          (C.values x).getD u false)) ∧
+    ∀ g ∈ ((List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], [])).1, ∃ k, ∀ a ∈ g.args,
+      C.layerNAND.depthAt a = k := by
+  induction v with
+  | zero => exact ⟨fun u hu => absurd hu (Nat.not_lt_zero u), fun g hg => by simp at hg⟩
+  | succ v ih =>
+    have hst := C.layerFold_struct v
+    rw [List.range_succ, List.foldl_append, List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_nil] at hpre ⊢
+    generalize (List.range v).foldl C.layerColumn ([], []) = s at ih hst hpre ⊢
+    obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, -⟩ := hst
+    obtain ⟨⟨col, hc, -⟩, hp, -, -, -⟩ := C.layerColumn_struct s v h2 h3 h4
